A rejected file exits 3, writes nothing to standard output, and writes one diagnostic line to standard error naming the file and, for a ragged record, its line number.\n\n## Rejection Rules\n\n1. The file is empty (no records at all).\n2. A header name is empty after trimming.\n3. A header name repeats another after trimming (exact comparison).\n4. Any data record has a different number of fields from the header.\n\nField-count checking is done here, not by `encoding/csv`, so the diagnostic can name the offending line. A header-only file (header and no records) is valid and is not rejected.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validation runs before any statistic is computed, so no partial output is ever written.\n- Exit code is 3 for every rejection above.\n- Standard output is empty on rejection.\n- Standard error carries one diagnostic line with the file path and, for a ragged record, the line number.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC reject-ragged-fixture ===\nIntent: The supplied ragged fixture is rejected with exit 3 and nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; This story owns the accepted forms; the rejection of invalid forms belongs to the usage-errors story.\n\n## Command Forms\n\n```text\ncsvstat [--column <name>] <file>\ncsvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>\n```\n\n| Option | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `--column <name>` | Profile only the named column. |\n| `--freq <name>` | List the most frequent values of the named column instead of profiling. |\n| `--top <n>` | With `--freq`, list at most `<n>` values. A positive integer; default `5`. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Options precede exactly one file argument.\n- With no option, every column is profiled.\n- `--column <name>` produces a profile containing only that column.\n- `--freq <name>` produces the frequency list with header `value,count`.\n- `--top` defaults to 5 when `--freq` is given without it.\n- The parsed request carries: file path, column name (optional), freq name (optional), and top count.\n- Only `main` calls `os.Exit`; `Run` returns the exit code.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC options-no-option-profiles-all ===\nIntent: With only a file argument, every column is profiled in file order.\nRequires: executable=go; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-RejectMalformed.md ===\n# FEATURE: Reject Malformed Input\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Reject empty files, empty or repeated header names, and ragged records with exit code 3 and no standard output. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-NullTrim.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | exit 3 on rejected input |\n| Consumes    | reader.Read, reader.Trim |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAfter the file is read and trimmed, csvstat validates its shape. A rejected file exits 3, writes nothing to standard output, and writes one diagnostic line to standard error naming the file and, for a ragged record, its line number.\n\n## Rejection Rules\n\n1. The file is empty (no records at all).\n2. A header name is empty after trimming.\n3. A header name repeats another after trimming (exact comparison).\n4. Any data record has a different number of fields from the header.\n\nField-count checking is done here, not by `encoding/csv`, so the diagnostic can name the offending line. A header-only file (header and no records) is valid and is not rejected.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Validation runs before any statistic is computed, so no partial output is ever written.\n- Exit code is 3 for every rejection above.\n- Standard output is empty on rejection.\n- Standard error carries one diagnostic line with the file path and, for a ragged record, the line number.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC reject-ragged-fixture ===\nIntent: The supplied ragged fixture is rejected with exit 3 and nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"sources/ragged.csv\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 3, (code, out, err)\nassert out == \"\", out\n=== END AC reject-ragged-fixture ===\n\n=== AC reject-empty-file ===\nIntent: A zero-byte file is rejected with exit 3 and nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(work, \"empty.csv\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    handle.write(\"\")\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 3, (code, out, err)\nassert out == \"\", out\n=== END AC reject-empty-file ===\n\n=== AC reject-empty-header-name ===\nIntent: A header with an empty or blank name is rejected with exit 3.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nfor label, header in ((\"blank\", \"a, ,c\"), (\"empty\", \"a,,c\")):\n    path = os.path.join(work, label + \".csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n        handle.write(header + \"\\n1,2,3\\n\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(label, code, out, err)\n    assert code == 3, (label, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (label, out)\n=== END AC reject-empty-header-name ===\n\n=== AC reject-repeated-header-name ===\nIntent: A repeated header name, including one that repeats only after trimming, is rejected with exit 3.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nfor label, header in ((\"plain\", \"a,b,a\"), (\"padded\", \"a,b, a \")):\n    path = os.path.join(work, label + \".csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n        handle.write(header + \"\\n1,2,3\\n\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(label, code, out, err)\n    assert code == 3, (label, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (label, out)\n=== END AC reject-repeated-header-name ===\n\n=== AC reject-short-and-long-records ===\nIntent: A record with too few fields and a record with too many fields are each rejected with exit 3.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\ncases = {\n    \"short\": \"a,b,c\\n1,2,3\\n4,5\\n\",\n    \"long\": \"a,b,c\\n1,2,3\\n4,5,6,7\\n\",\n}\nfor label, body in cases.items():\n    path = os.path.join(work, label + \".csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n        handle.write(body)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(label, code, out, err)\n    assert code == 3, (label, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (label, out)\n=== END AC reject-short-and-long-records ===\n\n=== AC accept-header-only ===\nIntent: A header-only file is valid and is not rejected.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"sources/header_only.csv\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n=== END AC accept-header-only ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- The standard error diagnostic for a ragged record names the file and the line number of the offending record.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A rejected file never writes anything to standard output.\n- Invalid UTF-8 bytes are not a rejection condition.\n- The rejection list is exhaustive: empty file, empty header name, repeated header name, ragged record, and (in other stories) an absent column name.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-UnreadableFile.md ===\n# FEATURE: Unreadable File\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Exit 1 with nothing on standard output when the input file cannot be opened or read. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-NullTrim.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | exit 1 on unreadable file |\n| Consumes    | reader.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn operational failure to open or read the file is distinct from a malformed file. It exits 1, writes nothing to standard output, and writes one diagnostic line to standard error naming the file.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- An error from opening the file exits 1.\n- An error while reading the file's bytes exits 1.\n- Standard output is empty in both cases.\n- Standard error carries one diagnostic line naming the file path.\n- Read errors are never reported as exit 3; exit 3 is reserved for content that was read but rejected.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC unreadable-missing-file ===\nIntent: A path that does not exist exits 1 with nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\nmissing = os.path.join(work, \"no-such-file.csv\")\nassert not os.path.exists(missing)\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(missing)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 1, (code, out, err)\nassert out == \"\", out\n=== END AC unreadable-missing-file ===\n\n=== AC unreadable-directory-path ===\nIntent: A path that names a directory cannot be read as a file and exits 1 with nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(work)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 1, (code, out, err)\nassert out == \"\", out\n=== END AC unreadable-directory-path ===\n\n=== AC unreadable-not-confused-with-rejected ===\nIntent: A readable but malformed file exits 3, not 1, so the two failure classes stay distinct.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"sources/ragged.csv\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code != 1, (code, out, err)\nassert code == 3, (code, out, err)\n=== END AC unreadable-not-confused-with-rejected ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- The standard error diagnostic for an unreadable file names the file path.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- An unreadable file never writes anything to standard output.\n- The file is read once; no partial profile is written before a read error is detected.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliOptions.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Options\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Parse the --column, --freq, and --top options and the single file argument into a request value. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | --column, --freq, --top |\n| Consumes    | cli.Run |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`internal/cli` parses arguments with a local `flag.FlagSet` created with `flag.ContinueOnError` and produces a request value that the profile and frequency stories consume. This story owns the accepted forms; the rejection of invalid forms belongs to the usage-errors story.\n\n## Command Forms\n\n```text\ncsvstat [--column <name>] <file>\ncsvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>\n```\n\n| Option | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `--column <name>` | Profile only the named column. |\n| `--freq <name>` | List the most frequent values of the named column instead of profiling. |\n| `--top <n>` | With `--freq`, list at most `<n>` values. A positive integer; default `5`. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Options precede exactly one file argument.\n- With no option, every column is profiled.\n- `--column <name>` produces a profile containing only that column.\n- `--freq <name>` produces the frequency list with header `value,count`.\n- `--top` defaults to 5 when `--freq` is given without it.\n- The parsed request carries: file path, column name (optional), freq name (optional), and top count.\n- Only `main` calls `os.Exit`; `Run` returns the exit code.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC options-no-option-profiles-all ===\nIntent: With only a file argument, every column is profiled in file order.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nnames = [\"alpha\", \"beta\", \"gamma\"]\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(work, \"data.csv\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    handle.write(\",\".join(names) + \"\\n1,2,3\\n4,5,6\\n\")\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert [row[0] for row in rows[1:]] == names, rows\n=== END AC options-no-option-profiles-all ===\n\n=== AC options-column-profiles-one ===\nIntent: --column profiles exactly the named column.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nnames = [\"alpha\", \"beta\", \"gamma\"]\nchosen = names[1]\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(work, \"data.csv\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    handle.write(\",\".join(names) + \"\\n1,2,3\\n4,5,6\\n\")\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--column\", chosen, path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) == 2, rows\nassert rows[1][0] == chosen, rows\n=== END AC options-column-profiles-one ===\n\n=== AC options-freq-default-top ===\nIntent: --freq without --top lists at most five values.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndefault_top = 5\ndistinct = 8\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(work, \"data.csv\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    handle.write(\"v\\n\")\n    for index in range(distinct):\n        handle.write(\"item%d\\n\" % index)\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"v\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) - 1 == default_top, rows\n=== END AC options-freq-default-top ===\n\n=== AC options-freq-explicit-top ===\nIntent: --top limits the frequency list to the requested number of rows.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nlimit = 2\ndistinct = 6\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(work, \"data.csv\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    handle.write(\"v\\n\")\n    for index in range(distinct):\n        handle.write(\"item%d\\n\" % index)\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"v\", \"--top\", str(limit), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) - 1 == limit, rows\n=== END AC options-freq-explicit-top ===\n\n=== AC options-freq-top-larger-than-distinct ===\nIntent: A --top larger than the number of distinct values lists every distinct value.\nRequires: executable=go; The name is matched against the trimmed header names, exactly and case-sensitively. A name absent from the header is a rejected input.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The `--column` value is compared to each trimmed header name by exact, case-sensitive string equality.\n- A match produces the profile header plus one row for that column only, with statistics computed exactly as in the full profile.\n- No match exits 3, writes nothing to standard output, and writes one diagnostic to standard error naming the file and the missing column.\n- The check runs after the file has been read and validated, so a malformed file still exits 3 for its own reason.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC column-select-one-row ===\nIntent: --column outputs the profile header and a single row for the named column.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nactual = \"qty\"\nwork = tempfile.mkdtemp()\npath = os.path.join(work, \"data.csv\")\nwith open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    handle.write(\"id,\" + actual + \"\\n1,10\\n\")\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"--column\", actual.upper(), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 3, (code, out, err)\nassert out == \"\", out\n=== END AC column-select-case-sensitive ===\n\n=== AC column-select-trimmed-header ===\nIntent: A header name padded with spaces in the file is matched by its trimmed name.\nRequires: executable=go; there is no case folding or prefix matching.\n- An absent column never produces partial standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-UsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: Usage Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Reject invalid command lines with exit 2, nothing on standard output, and both synopses on standard error. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CliOptions.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | exit 2 on usage errors |\n| Consumes    | --column, --freq, --top |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAny malformed command line exits 2 before the file is opened. Standard output stays empty, and standard error receives both synopses so the user can see the valid forms.\n\n## Usage Errors\n\n- No file argument, or more than one file argument.\n- An unknown option, including `--help` and `-h` (no help flag is defined).\n- An option that requires a value but has none.\n- `--top` whose value is not a positive integer (zero, negative, or non-numeric).\n- `--top` given without `--freq`.\n- `--column` given together with `--freq`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Exit code is 2 for every case above.\n- Standard output is empty.\n- Standard error receives both synopses: `csvstat [--column <name>] <file>` and `csvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>`.\n- Usage errors are decided before the file is opened, so a nonexistent file never turns a usage error into exit 1.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-missing-or-extra-file ===\nIntent: No file argument and two file arguments each exit 2 with nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncases = {\n    \"none\": [],\n    \"two\": [\"sources/orders.csv\", \"sources/sensors.csv\"],\n}\nfor label, args in cases.items():\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n    print(label, code, out, err)\n    assert code == 2, (label, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (label, out)\n=== END AC usage-missing-or-extra-file ===\n\n=== AC usage-unknown-option ===\nIntent: An unknown option, including --help and -h, exits 2 with nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nfor flag in (\"--frobnicate\", \"--help\", \"-h\"):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(flag, \"sources/orders.csv\")\n    print(flag, code, out, err)\n    assert code == 2, (flag, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (flag, out)\n=== END AC usage-unknown-option ===\n\n=== AC usage-missing-option-value ===\nIntent: Options that need a value but have none exit 2 with nothing on standard output.\nRequires: executable=go;
